a573441d9829d9a6fa789afbcd2e14e7407e208d [ diff ] 
[Actor] Auto-wrap ExecuteJs scripts and report sync JS errors

Previously, ExecuteJsTool ran raw script code via Blink's
ExecuteScriptInIsolatedWorldAndReturnValue, which failed on bare
`return` or top-level `await` statements and swallowed synchronous
exception and syntax error details instead of populating
js_error_message.

Compile and run scripts directly in the actor isolated world context
with v8::Script::Compile under a v8::TryCatch and v8::MicrotasksScope.
When raw compilation fails, retry wrapped as an async arrow expression
and then as an async arrow block so bare `await`, bare `return`, and
unparenthesized object literals succeed automatically. Extract caught V8
error messages and adjusted line numbers into ActionResult::message and
the ExecuteJsResponse js_error_message field.

Revert CHECK_LE to DCHECK_LE in BitSet32::get_index_of_bit

Revert the CHECK_LE assertion in BitSet32::get_index_of_bit back to a
DCHECK_LE. The CHECK macro was causing crashes in non-debug builds when
the bit index exceeded 31. Reverting to DCHECK_LE avoids crashing in
production builds while retaining the assertion during debug runs.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/695f8504af800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Scope Storage cookie commands to the target's partition

Non-browser targets fell back to `GetDefaultBrowserContext()`, which
would return the last-used profile.

Targets with no partition now get InternalError instead of a result from
the wrong profile. Pages in a non-default partition now use their own
partition.

This change is based on the patch provided in the bug report

parent
3f78e935a5cb816be9b60c9539dcb82d70f5de94 [ diff ] 
[Android] Create background Navigate() WebContents hidden

When Navigate() opens a kNewBackgroundTab on Android without an
existing contents_to_insert, CreateNewTab() previously constructed a
default WebContents::CreateParams with initially_hidden = false.
Because WebContentsImpl::UpdateWebContentsVisibility() ignores HIDDEN
transitions before the first real VISIBLE transition, unselected
background tabs created through Navigate() (such as Send-Tab-To-Self
auto-opened tabs) remained in Visibility::VISIBLE until first
activated by the user.

Set create_params.initially_hidden = true when disposition is
kNewBackgroundTab, matching desktop CreateTargetContents().

[actor] Support ResumeTask in transport handler and adapter

Routes ResumeTaskRequest payloads in ActuationTransportHandler through
ActuationDelegate::ResumeTask to ActorKeyedServiceAdapter, which resumes
paused tasks, clears any pending takeover result, and collects fresh
surface observations according to ObservationConfig.

ActuationDelegate::PauseTask to ActorKeyedServiceAdapter, which pauses
active tasks using the requested pause reason and replies with
PauseTaskResponse.

in a follow-up CL.

[WebView] Add ProcessHtmlDataImmediately to supported flags

android_webview/docs/developer-ui.md recommends listing features that
run field trials on WebView in ProductionSupportedFlagList. Add
ProcessHtmlDataImmediately so that a field trial can evaluate it on
WebView. The feature has no effect unless its params are set.

Also add android_webview to the ProcessHtmlDataImmediately entry in
fieldtrial_testing_config.json so that the testing config also covers
WebView.

c78fb51bd417e923f5a99d84bd661119048334df
parent
0103ca1cd019a9c43f74d1933b76648a49b55d25 [ diff ] 
on_device_model: Plumb MTP drafter weight and program cache files

LiteRT-LM isolated MTP drafter caches from the main model, so Chrome's
sandboxed service must pass dedicated file descriptors to cache drafter
weights and shaders across cold starts.

Plumb mtp_drafter_cache.bin and mtp_drafter_program_cache.bin through
ModelAssets and Mojo into ChromeMLModelData for a follow-up ChromeML CL.
Gate these files on the disabled-by-default
OnDeviceModelSpeculativeDecoding flag (and existing cache flags) so
default behavior and disk usage are unchanged.

Disallow dedicated worker creation for inactive documents
📋 Metadata
commit
cad1925e8563ccb14499152ee2787b6b607a37f2
[ log ]
author
Alex Attar <aattar@google.com>
Fri Nov 13 06:44:01 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 13 06:44:01 2026
tree
d9d6f9585f42b309318c0a394a4cc0614eeb8ffa
parent
69999e94d4dfd7ed632540850d7aece7af32c7ba [ diff ] 
Disallow dedicated worker creation for inactive documents

When a document enters the back/forward cache, existing dedicated
workers are paused. However, if a Mojo request to bind a
DedicatedWorkerHostFactory, start a worker script load, or create a
nested dedicated worker arrives after the frame becomes inactive, a new
worker could be initialized in an unpaused state.

Check IsInactiveAndDisallowActivation() with
DisallowActivationReasonId::kCreateDedicatedWorker in
RenderFrameHostImpl::CreateDedicatedWorkerHostFactory(),
DedicatedWorkerHostFactoryImpl::CreateWorkerHostAndStartScriptLoad(),
and DedicatedWorkerHost::CreateNestedDedicatedWorker() so requests for
cached documents disallow activation and evict the page from the
back/forward cache.

[wtf] Always use CHECK_EQ in UncheckedIterator::CheckModifications()

UncheckedIterator::CheckModifications() is already guarded by
kCheckModifications, so using DCHECK_EQ when DCHECK_IS_ON() is true and
CHECK_EQ otherwise is redundant. Always use CHECK_EQ.

Revert CHECK back to DCHECK in SessionRestoreImpl

The conversion of DCHECK to CHECK in SessionRestoreImpl caused crash
failures when multiple restores occur. Revert this check back to a
DCHECK with a CHECK-exclusion comment until it can be verified that it
will not be triggered unexpectedly in legitimate scenarios.

22b635e80890d22b97e826c98c93e7e008e4ef52 [ diff ] 
Revert CHECK to DCHECK in PeriodicBackgroundSyncPermissionContext

In GetContentSettingStatusInternal within
PeriodicBackgroundSyncPermissionContext, a CHECK verifies that
content_setting is either CONTENT_SETTING_BLOCK or
CONTENT_SETTING_ALLOW. However, when content_setting evaluates to
another value (such as CONTENT_SETTING_ASK), this CHECK triggers a
crash.

This change reverts the CHECK macro back to DCHECK and adds the CHECK-
exclusion TODO comment to prevent crashes when unexpected content
settings are returned.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/1bc993b088400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

parent
4a0cb047f51667e69ca032c971c654eb6eb8d1f6 [ diff ] 
ttc: End the session when the active ChromeOS user switches

On ChromeOS, switching to another signed-in user or opening the
multi-profile sign-in screen leaves the previous user's browser alive in
the background without locking the screen, so a TTC session kept the
microphone open under the new user's desktop.

Extend the lock and suspend detector on ChromeOS to observe
UserManager::UserSessionStateObserver::ActiveUserChanged() and
SessionManagerObserver::OnSessionStateChanged(), following
MultiCaptureSessionController. SessionControllerImpl logs the new
kUserSwitch reason and ends the session through EndSessionAsync()
without an error toast. On ChromeOS the LOCKED session state now reports
the screen lock, replacing ui::AddScreenLockCallback(), whose D-Bus
signal arrives later. The detector is renamed from LockOrSuspendDetector
to SessionEndConditionDetector, since it now covers more than lock and
suspend.

[Public Transit] Make the toolbar a default Facility of CtaPageStation

The toolbar Elements of CtaPageStation (control_container, tab switcher,
menu, home, mic and sign-in buttons) move to a new ToolbarFacility,
which CtaPageStation enters by default with declareDefaultFacility().
CtaPageStation keeps its toolbar fields, forwarded from the Facility, so
callers don't change.

This is a first step to model toolbar states the Stations can't express
today (e.g. scrolled off or hidden), which callers will be able to enter
instead of the default one.

No behavior change: the same Elements are checked in the same Trips.

parent
0ab684941fc27544a1f46ab1537a88a2d86f0842 [ diff ] 
quick_pair: Don't duplicate persisted device images in memory

DeviceImageStore copied every persisted image (base64 PNG data URLs)
from local state prefs into |model_id_to_images_|, although prefs
already keep them in memory. Keep only not-yet-persisted images in
the map, read persisted ones from prefs on demand, and save images
fetched after persisting directly to prefs.

Also bound |model_id_to_images_| with base::LRUCache (20 models,
matching FastPairRepositoryImpl::kMaxMetadataCacheSize) for discovered
devices that are never paired, and keep the images in the map
zstd-compressed, decompressing them when retrieved or persisted. The
prefs format is unchanged.

CONV=6d264d6a-5054-4cf4-8eeb-2d561d8124cc

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in BrowserWindowCommandHandler commandDispatch

In commandDispatch, was_executed can be false after ExecuteCommand. The
CHECK macro was triggering crashes in release builds when was_executed
was false. Reverting the CHECK back to a DCHECK prevents these non-fatal
failures from crashing the browser while investigation continues.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/a54478f088400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

0803ee383e0af5f51b19a06dd35fb529ac46b15a [ diff ] 
[Gardener] Disable ArtemisLogSourceTest.VerifyNewLinesAppearAfterRefresh

The test is consistently failing since https://crrev.com/c/8807214.
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/:%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C:unit_tests%21gtest::ArtemisLogSourceTest%23VerifyNewLinesAppearAfterRefresh

Failure Link:
- https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/linux-cfm-rel/93872/overview
- https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/linux-cfm-rel/93877/overview

Gemini's diagnosis:
ArtemisLogSourceTest.VerifyNewLinesAppearAfterRefresh has been
consistently failing on linux-cfm-rel since the libc++ roll in
https://crrev.com/c/8807214 (reland of https://crrev.com/c/8717169;
specifically upstream libc++ commit 9b1a975d64: "[libc++] Optimize
filebuf::seekoff (#211788)").

In LogFile::RetrieveNextLogs(), reading the first line via
std::getline(file_stream_, line) triggers basic_filebuf::underflow(),
which calls std::fread(..., 4096, __file_). Because the test file only
has 10 lines, std::fread performs a short read and sets the EOF
indicator on the underlying C FILE*. Previously, calling
file_stream_.tellg() after each line invoked seekoff(0, ios_base::cur)
-> sync() -> fseek(), which cleared the FILE* EOF indicator. After
9b1a975d64, seekoff(0, ios_base::cur) computes the offset from buffer
pointers without calling fseek(), so the underlying FILE* retains its
EOF flag and subsequent reads after appending lines without Refresh()
(case #2 in the test) immediately hit EOF.

TAG=agy
CONV=e44bb9fb-d3b5-4644-add3-36c95b89b409

parent
2bc56998ae052dee20012cdb8641fd518387566b [ diff ] 
updater: Remove quarantine from the wake plist before bootstrapping it

launchd on macOS 27 no longer loads launchd plists that carry
com.apple.quarantine (166415497):
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/macos-release-notes/macos-27-release-notes#Launch-Daemons-and-Agents

The browser sets LSFileQuarantineEnabled, so an updater it launches
(--install from InstallUpdaterAndRegisterBrowser, --wake-all from
WakeAllUpdaters) inherits file quarantine and writes a quarantined wake
plist. launchctl bootstrap then fails with error 5 (EIO), and setup
fails with kErrorFailedToCreateWakeLaunchdJobPlist (27). On a machine
without an updater, this leaves the browser with no updater at all.

DoSetup dequarantines only the install dir; the plist is written outside
it. EnsureWakeLaunchItemPresence also skips an unchanged plist, so a
plist quarantined by an earlier browser-launched run (accepted by macOS
26) is never repaired and is dropped by launchd at login on 27.

- BootstrapPlist removes the quarantine attribute before bootstrapping,
  in both scopes. The backup-restore retry goes through it too.
- EnsureWakeLaunchItemPresence rewrites an unchanged plist if it is
  quarantined.

Tested with new Mac integration tests that use the real launchctl:
- OverinstallBrokenKeepsWakePlist: an unchanged plist is left alone.
- OverinstallBrokenRepairsQuarantinedWakePlist: an unchanged quarantined
  plist whose job launchd dropped is rewritten and reloaded.
- InstallWithInheritedQuarantine: --install from an app with
  LSFileQuarantineEnabled, as the browser does, ends with a clean plist
  and a loaded wake job.
Without the fix the last two fail; on macOS 27, --install exits 27.

Don't end recognition when SODA is released for silence

Destroying a running SODA instance after 10s of silence causes SODA to
emit a STOP event, which prematurely ends continuous on-device Web
Speech sessions. This CL unregisters the SODA client callback before
destroying the instance for silence and notifies the client directly if
MarkDone() is called while SODA is released.

[iOS Blink] Always run the FetchForms result callback

CallJavaScriptFunction() cannot run anything on the iOS Blink port yet,
so it returns false and destroys the result callback. FetchForms()
ignored the return value, so AutofillAgent never ran the completion of
its suggestion availability query, which stalled callers on field focus.

This CL posts a missing result when the call doesn't go through, like
the other ports do when the call times out.

TEST: AutofillAgentTest.CheckIfSuggestionsAvailable_FormExtractionFailed
      AutofillAgentTest.CheckIfSuggestionsAvailable_MultipleFormsExtracted
      AutofillAgentTest.CheckIfSuggestionsAvailable_NoFormExtracted
      AutofillAgentTest.CheckIfSuggestionsAvailable_PriorInFlightQueryPreserved

tree
7a4c42bed0837e39d4d4965e6baac56f78a5df68
parent
c9835f10ad38c849dbc3503126b25100deb8fe67 [ diff ] 
[Speech] Drop buffered downstream chunks on abort

NetworkSpeechRecognitionEngineImpl::OnDownstreamDataReceived() dispatches
one EVENT_DOWNSTREAM_RESPONSE per protocol chunk in a loop. If a chunk
carries an error status (or fails to parse), ProcessDownstreamResponse()
calls Abort(), which moves the FSM to STATE_IDLE but leaves the
remaining chunks in |chunked_byte_buffer_|. The loop then dispatches the
next chunk in STATE_IDLE, which hits NotFeasible() and crashes the
browser process with:

  NOTREACHED hit. Unfeasible event 6 in state 0

Clear the buffer in Abort(). This ends the dispatch loop once recognition
is aborted, and it also stops a partial chunk from a previous session
being prepended to the next session's downstream data.

parent
0fdd41982f29a5469e0fb9f65487baccfa052da3 [ diff ] 
blink: Add simdutf fast path to TextCodecUtf8::Decode

TextCodecUtf8::Decode currently optimistically allocates an 8-bit
buffer sized to the full UTF-8 input length and copies the ASCII
prefix before encountering any code point above U+00FF. On large
streamed HTML or script chunks with late multi-byte UTF-8 sequences,
this discards the 8-bit buffer, allocates a second over-sized 16-bit
buffer, widens the already-copied prefix, and decodes the remaining
multi-byte sequences one character at a time.

Add TryDecodeWithSimdutf() behind kSimdutfTextCodecUtf8Decode
(disabled by default, enabled in fieldtrial_testing_config.json) for
chunks >= 256 bytes:
- Complete any pending 1..3 byte partial_sequence_ from the start of
  the chunk and split off any valid incomplete 1..3 byte trailing
  UTF-8 prefix when streaming, deferring codec state updates until
  decoding succeeds.
- Scan the ASCII prefix with base::FindFirstNonASCII() and return an
  8-bit String directly on pure-ASCII chunks without simdutf dispatch.
- Validate any non-ASCII suffix once via simdutf::validate_utf8().
- Preserve 8-bit String representation in a single exact-sized
  StringBuffer<LChar> when no byte in the suffix is >= 0xC4, avoiding
  wasted 8-bit allocations when Latin-1 precedes UTF-16 content.
- Otherwise allocate a single exact-sized StringBuffer<UChar> via
  simdutf::utf16_length_from_utf8() and transcode via
  simdutf::convert_utf8_to_utf16().
- Fall back to the scalar decoder on malformed UTF-8 so replacement
  character, maximal subpart, and stop_on_error behavior are unchanged.

In wtf_perf_tests, this speeds up 64 KB late-UTF-16 decoding by 15.7x,
32 KB Latin-1 decoding by 6.6x, 32 KB Latin-1-before-UTF-16 decoding
by 2.3x, and 64 KB split-UTF-8 4 KB chunk streaming by 2.2x with no
pure-ASCII regression. On interleaved A/B page-load benchmarks (n=38
pairs), cumulative TextResourceDecoder::Decode drops by 11.3% (-0.32
ms, p = 3e-6) and main-thread HTML chunk decoding drops by 39.0%.

77fe75ed86fb9bcafd47dfb822da7017e4044e95 [ diff ] 
[Linux/X11] Exclude CSD frame insets from browser window bounds

On X11 window managers that support _GTK_FRAME_EXTENTS (Mutter, Muffin,
KWin), Chrome's X window includes the client-drawn shadow and resize
border, and the window manager only treats the area inside the
published extents as the window. All BrowserWindow bounds used the
X window rect, so chrome.windows.update({left: 0, top: 0}) put the
visible frame at the frame insets (e.g. 22,19 on Cinnamon), and
requested sizes came out smaller by the insets. The same applied to
DevTools Browser.setWindowBounds, ChromeDriver, and to
window.screenX/screenY and window.outerWidth/outerHeight.

Translate between the widget bounds and the window bounds exposed
through BrowserWindow: BrowserView::GetBounds(), GetRestoredBounds()
and SetBounds(), and the saved window placement, now exclude the frame
insets that X11Window publishes in _GTK_FRAME_EXTENTS (only when the
window manager supports them). Maximized and fullscreen windows have
no insets; their restored bounds use the insets that were published
before the window left the normal state, since the frame layout
depends on the tiled state, which X11 window managers also set for
maximized windows. Internal users of widget bounds (the root view's
preferred size, the fullscreen-on-display path, a window that is
launched maximized, and dragging a tab into a new window) are
converted accordingly.

Add aura::WindowTreeHost::GetInvisibleFrameInsetsInDIP() (empty by
default), implement it for Linux browser windows with the same insets,
and exclude them in RenderWidgetHostViewAura::
GetBoundsInScreenWithoutTransform(), which provides the window rect
sent to the renderer, and in GetBoundsInScreen(), which
window.moveTo() and window.resizeTo() combine with the requested
origin or size before calling BrowserView::SetBounds(). Both halves
are in one change so that neither can land or be reverted alone:
with only the BrowserView part, every moveTo() would grow a window by
the insets and every resizeTo() would move it by the top-left insets.

Window placements saved by older versions are read as window bounds,
so such windows restore once larger by the insets.

Tested on Xorg + Muffin 6.6.3: chrome.windows.update() to 0,0 800x600
now shows the frame at 0,0 with size 800x600 and the page reports
screenX/screenY 0,0 and outerWidth/outerHeight 800x600, the window
placement round-trips across restarts, and Openbox (no
_GTK_FRAME_EXTENTS support) is unchanged.

android: Roll Android 17 AVDs with lavapipe

configs to use lavapipe (host Vulkan-on-CPU over Gfxstream Vulkan)
following https://crrev.com/c/8798270, avoiding the ~34% software GL
translation overhead of swangle_indirect on 8-core bots.

Disabled some failure tests due to a gfxstream bug on the emulator side.

🌺 Revert "[TTC] Add PointerContextCapturer for get_pointer"

This reverts commit 9ffd399ae56d3f6c6163a05cdba7284f20367935.
Culprit: 9ffd399ae56d - "[TTC] Add PointerContextCapturer for get_pointer" (refs/heads/main@{#1743591}, https://crrev.com/c/8795395)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-14-arm64-rel.

Mechanism: Breaks
PointerContextCapturerScaleBrowserTest#CapturesScreenshotAndPageContent/All.1
across 5 CI builders (7 failures, including 2 consecutive hard fails on
android-14-arm64-rel). The new test uses strict ASSERT_EQ on
view->GetDeviceScaleFactor() vs GetParam() (1.50000012 != 1.5) instead
of EXPECT_NEAR / ASSERT_FLOAT_EQ.

PointerContextCapturerScaleBrowserTest#CapturesScreenshotAndPageContent/All.1
has failed in 7/7 recent builds of android-14-arm64-rel, every attempt
in every one of them. This is not flake: it reproduces on every run, so
it blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that touches this suite. The
regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1743591}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665289570493226801

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: *|chrome_public_test_apk

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [TTC] Add PointerContextCapturer for get_pointer
>
> To answer "what is this?", get_pointer needs two things from the page:
> a screenshot to crop the image of the pointed-at spot from, and the
> page content to find the element at that spot in.
> PointerContextCapturer fetches both with a single PageContextFetcher
> request, so that they describe the same moment and the element found
> is the one in the image.
>
> The screenshot is the viewport at its natural size, barely compressed
> since the image made from it is compressed again, and the page content
> is extracted in actionable mode, which has geometry in device pixels;
> the capturer reports the device scale factor, which then converts the
> user's DIP positions for both.
>
> The user is waiting on a spoken answer and captures can stall, e.g.
> while the window is occluded, so the capturer gives up after a timeout
> chosen by its caller and reports kToolTimeout. Failures are reported
> as the ToolResponse to answer the tool call with, using the codes
> get_screenshot uses; the mapping from fetch errors moves to
> ToScreenshotErrorCode() to be shared.
>
> Nothing uses the capturer yet; the tool is assembled in a future CL,
> which also checks that the page's URL may be captured, as
> get_screenshot does.
>
> Design: go/ttc-get-pointer (CL C2 of the plan)
>
>     --gtest_filter=*PointerContextCapturer*
>
> Bug: 571268337
> Test: browser_tests / android_browsertests

2fb19577dd7f01ce332b7c810192a5ba67e19c21 [ diff ] 
[Android] Fix null BrowserContext crash in DownloadController

When a download is started or resumed in Android Reduced Mode (or before
DownloadManagerImpl imports it and calls DownloadItemUtils::AttachInfo),
DownloadItemUtils::GetBrowserContext(item) is null and BrowserThread::UI
is not yet registered. Calling
GetBrowserContext(item)->GetDownloadManager() in
DownloadController::ScheduleRemoveDownloadItem entered
BrowserContext::GetDownloadManager() with this == nullptr, triggering
the UI thread check failure and a null pointer dereference.

Use
SimpleDownloadManagerCoordinatorFactory::GetForKey(GetProfileKey(item))
and post to SingleThreadTaskRunner::GetCurrentDefault() instead.

f30c0a5a33c0af6b2db9d0f178ba986639429d7b [ diff ] 
[unsafe-buffer] Remove unsafe buffer usage from RasterImplementationTest

The test wrapped memcpy/memset/memcmp and ring buffer pointer arithmetic
in UNSAFE_TODO. Use the span accessors the involved objects already
expose (Buffer::as_byte_span(), ExpectedMemoryInfo::span,
CommandBufferHelper::GetPutOffsetForTest()): the mock actions take spans
and command comparisons use bounds-checked slices. The YUV plane spans
come from gfx::SkPixmapToSpan() instead of raw pointer + size. GetPut()
now returns a span, and CommandsMatch() gains an overload taking it so
the hand-written comparisons share the generated tests' helper.

[webgpu] Pass DawnInstance into WebGPUDecoder::Create()

Move creation of the DawnInstance used by WebGPU contexts out of
WebGPUDecoder::Create() and into its callers so that the instance can
eventually be shared across WebGPU contexts. For now,
WebGPUCommandBufferStub, InProcessCommandBuffer, and WebGPUDecoderTest
all create it directly via DawnInstance::CreateForWebGPU().

DawnInstance is now exported from the gpu_gles2 component since it is
created from gpu_ipc_service.

There is no behavior change yet: each WebGPU context still gets its own
DawnInstance.

18cfc61b7e3d72ed41ce4e46c95c158d7900a177 [ diff ] 
[agents] Add shared code-review skill and reviewer agents

Previously, the Chromium owners code review skill and reviewer agent
lived inside `components/webapps/_agents/_harness/`. Because they were
quarantined inside the webapps harness, other teams could not easily
discover them, and the `security-review` skill lacked a dedicated
read-only subagent definition.

This change promotes the review workflow to `//agents/`:
1. Code review skill (`//agents/skills/code-review/`):
   - `SKILL.md` defines the 5-step Chromium owners review workflow for
     local changes or Gerrit CLs, with untrusted-input guardrails,
     bug/context fetching (`crbug` / `buganizer-ticket`), and delegation
     of security-sensitive changes to `security_reviewer`.
   - `references/gerrit_review.md` layers Gerrit-specific steps onto
     `SKILL.md` (`gerrit-cli` context and thread fetching, optional
     local prototyping in Step 6, and patchset re-reviews in Step 7 via
     `references/review-new-gerrit-patchset.md`).
2. Reviewer agents (`//agents/agents/`):
   - `code_reviewer.md` wraps `code-review` (loading
     `references/gerrit_review.md` for Gerrit CLs) and delegates
     security-sensitive changes to `security_reviewer`.
   - `security_reviewer.md` wraps
     `//agents/skills/security-review/SKILL.md` in a read-only subagent
     definition.
3. Harness and internal rule integration:
   - Updates `components/webapps/_agents/` manifests and docs to use the
     shared `//agents/` definitions and removes the old harness copies.
   - Wires `//internal/agents/rules/internal_instructions.md` into the
     agent definitions for Google-internal tool instructions.

Convert RGBAF16 CPU frames to 10-bit YUV for encoding
📋 Metadata
commit
18cfc61b7e3d72ed41ce4e46c95c158d7900a177
[ log ]
author
Zhibo Wang <zhibo1.wang@intel.com>
Fri Nov 13 05:19:11 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 13 05:19:11 2026
tree
9cddae90276fc4a29361f947150e744192459695
parent
249a574acd49e8107b91e6afcc51876a4cb6df36 [ diff ] 
Convert RGBAF16 CPU frames to 10-bit YUV for encoding

VideoFrameConverter now converts RGBAF16 to P010LE, P210LE and P410LE,
so an F16 frame in CPU memory can be encoded as HEVC Main10 or range
extension 4:2:2/4:4:4 10-bit. WebCodecs falls back to this when the GPU
cannot convert an F16 canvas, for example when Skia cannot write P010.

Since libyuv has no half float input, so Skia color manages the pixels
into the RGB form of the destination color space, which follows the
existing RGB rule. They are then converted at 10 bits with box filtered
chroma. The D3D12 encoder's shared memory upload now passes the
converted color space to the delegate instead of the frame's RGB one.

parent
091aab9abcbb2ce444de3ba95cc36cfea02efdf6 [ diff ] 
[chromecast] Validate PCM buffers and DRM subsample sizes

Reject encrypted or unaligned PCM audio configs and buffers in the
Starboard media backend and renderer, and return an empty buffer instead
of crashing on invalid or unsupported PCM resampler inputs. Also verify
that non-EOS buffers are non-empty and that DRM subsample byte counts
match the final buffer size before updating decoder state or writing
samples.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[linux] Drop the client-side frame border when tiled
📋 Metadata
commit
9098b43dfb3df310a6cf0ee0931e15ff9fb9673f
[ log ]
author
Thomas Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>
Fri Nov 13 04:46:53 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 13 04:46:53 2026
tree
3fbdba7c7dfc032482f4c82dea17a41bb5a40bac
parent
e4dec51e69500723bd69bde602f98f537ac90a7b [ diff ] 
[linux] Drop the client-side frame border when tiled

At fractional scales such as 1.75 and 1.25 on KWin, quick-tiled
browser windows leave a 1px column next to the tab strip and toolbar,
at the seam between the tiles and at the screen edge, where the
backdrop shows through.

The client-side frame keeps a transparent 10 DIP resize border even
when the window is tiled. At 1.75 that border is 17.5 px, so the
window geometry starts in the middle of a buffer pixel. Whichever way
the compositor rounds it, one edge column of the frame ends up
unpainted.

Drop the border insets (and the resize band in them) while the window
is tiled, like when it is maximized. The window geometry then starts
at the surface origin and stays aligned to the pixel grid. This
applies to the classic and GTK browser frames, the views frame and
the classic picture-in-picture frames. The GTK browser frame paints
its frame border outside the view when tiled, so the headerbar fills
the whole window.

Fixed: 575030919

crostini: Merge the import cases in OpenFileDialog()

ImportContainer() opens the file dialog with IMPORT, although the
chosen file may turn out to be a disk image backup, in which case
FileSelected() switches to IMPORT_DISK_IMAGE. So OpenFileDialog() must
handle both kinds of import the same, which so far was only implied by
two identical switch cases.

Merge the cases and say why. No change in behavior.

[NTP] Remove fixed width caps on small action chips behind a flag

https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTE3MjAyOTQ2MzQ2MTg4OHw5OWExN2MyZi1lNA

Small action chips capped the button at 208px and the title at 150px, so
labels were ellipsized long before the 45 characters a label may have.
'kNtpScaledActionChipsUncappedWidth' (off by default) overrides both
caps so a chip fits its label. Large chips and the container are
unchanged.

[🌷] Disable flaky ImeTest#testLongPressInputWhileComposingText

testLongPressInputWhileComposingText has flaked every day since at
least 11-01, about 100 flaky runs upstream. It fails directly with
"Mismatched selection start" on API 34 through 37 (android-14-x64-rel,
android-15-x64-rel, android-15-tablet-x64-rel, android-16-x64-rel,
android-17-x64-fyi-rel, android-canary-x64-fyi-rel), and also takes
cascading failures from other methods in the IME batch.

Since it flakes on API 34 as well, an API 35+ disable like
https://crrev.com/c/8791575 would not cover it, so disable it
everywhere.

Test history: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fcontent%2Fshell%2Fandroid%5C%3Acontent_shell_test_apk%21junit%3Aorg.chromium.content.browser.input%3AImeTest%23testLongPressInputWhileComposingText
Failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665424252044263505

ash: Move c/b/chromeos/platform_keys to c/b/ash/platform_keys
📋 Metadata
commit
0f3afc92f217db7c4c8830ee2e53f2e498d9af91
[ log ]
author
Georg Neis <neis@chromium.org>
Fri Nov 13 04:27:37 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 13 04:27:37 2026
tree
65e94c8473d11751fe924c2c66e49559c3a18822
parent
f2f16a276d5a8c8dd9d791cf3d2ec8f9b8f9f2eb [ diff ] 
ash: Move c/b/chromeos/platform_keys to c/b/ash/platform_keys

We want to use c/b/chromeos for browser features as opposed to OS/system
features.

This code backs the chrome.platformKeys and
chrome.enterprise.platformKeys extension APIs, which give extensions
access to the keys and client certificates in the ChromeOS key store
(the user and system-wide hardware tokens). These are OS APIs rather
than browser APIs:

- Both exist only on ChromeOS.
- chrome.enterprise.platformKeys is not available to arbitrary
  extensions. It is limited to extensions force-installed by enterprise
  policy, the Imprivata login screen extension and a few allowlisted
  extensions, which use it to generate keys and install certificates on
  the device.
- chrome.platformKeys lets extensions such as third-party VPN clients
  authenticate with these platform-managed certificates. For managed
  users, access to corporate keys is governed by the ChromeOS-only
  KeyPermissions policy.

The code is implemented on top of ash::KeystoreService, and ARC uses
ExtensionKeyPermissionsService to apply the KeyPermissions policy to
Android apps.

Also move the code from the chromeos and chromeos::platform_keys
namespaces to ash::platform_keys, the namespace used by most of the
code in that directory.

[GTK] Cache symbolic caption button icons across NavButtonProviderGtk

This CL caches decoded GTK3 caption button icons per `(type, scale)` in
a process-wide `NavButtonCache` shared across `NavButtonProviderGtk`
instances behind a disabled-by-default `GtkCacheNavButtonIcons` feature
flag (with a `fieldtrial_testing_config.json` entry) and populates it
from `PrefetchNavButtonIcons()`.

Previously, GTK3 `GtkIconInfo` cached symbolic icons only by exact CSS
foreground color and only while a `GtkIconInfo` remained alive.
Rendering caption buttons in a new foreground state (such as
`GTK_STATE_FLAG_BACKDROP` when an existing window loses focus to a newly
opened browser window, or `:hover` / `:active`) triggered synchronous
SVG decodes via `glycin-svg` on the UI thread (`gdk-pixbuf >= 2.43`).
Decoding each symbolic SVG once into a white mask and tinting it in
memory with `SkBlendMode::kSrcIn` (matching the existing GTK4 tinting
path) eliminates repeated SVG decodes and invalidates cleanly on GTK
theme and icon-theme changes.

Note that, like the existing GTK4 path, symbolic caption icons are now
tinted uniformly with the button's CSS foreground color rather than
resolving separate `.success`, `.warning`, or `.error` symbolic SVG
palette classes. Non-symbolic fallback icons remain untinted.

In a 32-repetition interleaved Crossbench `browser-startup` A/B
benchmark on Linux Release (`--gtk-theme`, custom Chrome frame and
`GtkPrefetchNavButtonIcons` enabled):
- `InitialWebUI.NewWindow.AllSources.WithExistingWindow.BrowserWindow.FirstPaint.FromConstructor2`:
  213.00 ms -> 132.00 ms (-80.50 ms / -37.79% paired median, 95% CI
  [-92.00, -76.00] ms; P90 232.40 ms -> 142.00 ms)
- `InitialWebUI.NewWindow.AllSources.WithExistingWindow.ReloadButton.FirstContentfulPaint.FromConstructor2`:
  213.00 ms -> 132.00 ms (-80.50 ms / -37.79% paired median, 95% CI
  [-92.00, -76.00] ms; P90 232.40 ms -> 142.00 ms)
- `Startup.BrowserWindow.FirstPaint`:
  433.00 ms -> 429.00 ms (-7.00 ms / -1.62%, 95% CI [-14.00, +4.00] ms;
  P90 448.90 ms -> 442.90 ms)
- `InitialWebUI.Startup.ReloadButton.FirstContentfulPaint`:
  765.50 ms -> 760.50 ms (-4.50 ms / -0.59%, 95% CI [-13.00, +6.02] ms;
  P90 783.90 ms -> 797.20 ms)
- `Startup.FirstWebContents.NonEmptyPaint3`:
  851.50 ms -> 848.00 ms (-1.50 ms / -0.18%, 95% CI [-8.00, +6.00] ms;
  P90 871.90 ms -> 880.60 ms)

[NTP Aurora V2] Hug the tiles in the tablet inside-MVT layout too

On tablets with the inside-MVT layout, the MVT card stretches across the
full Feed width when all the tiles fit, leaving large empty areas on
both sides of the AI Mode tile and the shortcuts.

crrev.com/c/8745416 did the adjustment for the beside-MVT row with
CONTAINER_WRAPS_TILES. This CL turns that on for inside-MVT as well, so
the MVT card is only as wide as its tiles and the NTP column centers it
under the search box. Phones and the other layouts are unchanged.

Video: http://screencast/cast/NTMzMzc1NzUzMDczNDU5MnwxZjYyNDc5Ny1hNQ

quick_pair: Fix unbounded growth and leaks in repository caches

- Bound FastPairRepositoryImpl::metadata_cache_ with base::LRUCache
  (20 models, covering the models a user pairs in a session plus
  nearby devices in pairing mode) and clear duplicate raw image bytes
  from the proto after decoding.
- Stop inserting empty entries via operator[] on lookup/persist misses
  in DeviceAddressMap and DeviceImageStore.
- Erase entries from in-memory maps in EvictMacAddressRecord and
  EvictDeviceImages instead of only removing from PrefService. A
  device of the same model that is being paired fetches the images
  again on pairing.

CONV=6d264d6a-5054-4cf4-8eeb-2d561d8124cc

[webium] Allow binding a user profile after navigation

Design: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1lzWRQwj2u4QItO-Wd3BbhoJbvc7dIxLFBpZeGumxEmw

Gated behind `TopChromeWebUISharedRenderer`. This is Phase 2 of
toolbar prerendering shares one prerendered toolbar across new
windows of every profile, Incognito and Guest included. Its
WebContents loads before it knows its user profile, so this CL lets
it bind the profile later: `webui::SetUserProfile()` binds it once,
before or after navigation.

A late bind recomputes WebPreferences through
`WebContents::OnWebPreferencesChanged()`. Until the bind,
`webui::GetUserProfile()` returns null, and code that reads the user
profile accepts its absence:
- `GetProfileForWebPreferences()` returns null for the WebUI toolbar,
  and its callers keep the content defaults.
- `ChromeContentBrowserClientWebUiPart`, `FaviconSource`, and
  `SanitizedImageSource` skip the profile instead of CHECKing.

The initial WebUI still clears its generic font maps, so a late bind
keeps the fonts.

Still enforced:
- A bound WebContents is never rebound.
- `GetProfileForWebPreferences()` CHECKs a missing user profile for
  every WebContents other than the WebUI toolbar.

The shared profile design lists the same need for early navigation [1].

[1]: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1jTJ4rAlNMS6aKZtfSdKh61sVWq9M6-XQfHKjNsoa4dU/edit

Bug: b:573441656, b:531575122
Test: out/Default/unit_tests --gtest_filter='*TopChromeWebUIUserProfile*'

[Feed] Add flag to sync feed visibility by form factor.

Turning the Discover feed off on one Android device doesn't carry over
to the user's other devices, nor to a device restored from a backup, so
users need to turn it off again. A follow-up CL will sync that choice
between devices of the same form factor behind this feature; this CL
adds the feature and its chrome://flags entry.
Screenshot: http://shortn/_ZbFCYeIso7.

[Glic] Remove the Android auto-submit invoke path

The Java service exposed an auto-submit invoke that only tests called,
backed by a JNI function and an Android-only passkey helper. This
removes the Java method, its native bridge, and the helper's passkey
friend entry. Desktop and actor callers of the native auto-submit
invoke are unchanged.

The integration tests now send their prompt through the prepopulated
prompt invoke, which reaches the web client the same way.

Bug: None

tree
15eba573a91756fd83eed3351848ef123934608d
parent
3749274b06ae788c670fcfdbb047da5d01a01190 [ diff ] 
media: Fix duplicate timeupdate reload race in lifetime-reload web test

encrypted-media-lifetime-reload.html appends to location.hash and calls
location.reload() inside onTimeUpdate without detaching the timeupdate
listener or pausing playback. Because location.reload() navigates
asynchronously, a second timeupdate event in the pre-reload document
sees location.hash == '#x' and calls test.done() mid-navigation, causing
TestRunner to dump an empty 1-byte page instead of testharness output.

Capture whether the document is already reloaded once at startup and
detach the timeupdate listener and pause playback before reloading or
finishing. This eliminates ~460 weekly web test failures on Windows and
Linux debug bots.

3749274b06ae788c670fcfdbb047da5d01a01190

tree
5aeb9f4fa426e17282bf5571423bba47e65259d3
parent
c90e32e54bf91b2f8d13dfefea679a375964f6bc [ diff ] 
crostini: Clean up notification when backup/restore fails early

Start() registers a notification controller for a disk image backup or
restore up front. When ExportDiskImage() or ImportDiskImage() failed
early, e.g. because StopVm() had failed, they ran the completion
callback directly instead of through AfterDiskImageOperation(), so the
notification got stuck and further backups and restores were blocked
for the rest of the session.

Bind AfterDiskImageOperation() in Start() instead, so that early
failures clean up too, and merge ExportDiskImage() and ImportDiskImage()
into StartDiskImageOperation(). Also fail on any non-SUCCESS StopVm()
result, not just VM_STOP_FAILED.

Like later backup failures, early ones now delete the target file.
This only affects an existing file that the user chose to replace.

Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=CrostiniExportImportTest*

swa: Inject VariationsService into MediaAppGuestUI and its config

Remove g_browser_process->variations_service() from
ChromeMediaAppGuestUIDelegate::GetFeatureAccessChecker by injecting
VariationsService* from AshWebUIConfigManager into
MediaAppGuestUIConfig and ash::MediaAppGuestUI:

  ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PreProfileInit
    -> AshWebUIConfigManager
      -> MediaAppGuestUIConfig
        -> ash::MediaAppGuestUI
          -> ChromeMediaAppGuestUIDelegate::GetFeatureAccessChecker
            -> specialized_features::FeatureAccessChecker

Both MediaAppGuestUIConfig and ash::MediaAppGuestUI observe
VariationsService::Observer::OnVariationsServiceDestroyed to reset their
observations during BrowserProcessImpl::StartTearDown.

TAG=agy

a7179ae26c72fa17bbedff43bdf7770862839f86
parent
9059853dc4a99ccac5f23c469b8653b51fd2588c [ diff ] 
component_updater: Use JniRawPtr in Java scheduler

UpdateScheduler previously stored the C++ BackgroundTaskUpdateScheduler
pointer as a raw long field. When the C++ instance was destroyed, the
Java singleton retained a dangling raw pointer.

Migrate mNativeScheduler to
JniRawPtr<NativeBackgroundTaskUpdateScheduler>. setNativeScheduler()
now accepts null and releases the previously held pointer, and
~BackgroundTaskUpdateScheduler() calls setNativeScheduler(nullptr) so
the pointer is released when the C++ instance is destroyed. Native
methods now take JniPtr<NativeBackgroundTaskUpdateScheduler>.

parent
59848cb51108f0f91728594ade2c357a847a91f0 [ diff ] 
[Cast] Fix and re-enable `VideoSenderTest.MockEncoderGoldenCase`

`VideoSenderTest.MockEncoderGoldenCase` was disabled on Linux MSan in
https://crrev.com/c/7735744 and ChromeOS MSan in
https://crrev.com/c/7738922 (https://crbug.com/500613219).

In https://crrev.com/c/7718344, `OpenscreenTestSenders` was updated to
use `MockSender`, whose default `EnqueueFrame` action invokes the gMock
method `MockOpenscreenEnvironment::SendPacket` with a `ByteView` of
`frame.data`. Because `VideoSenderTest` sets no `EXPECT_CALL` on
`SendPacket`, gMock logs the uninteresting call and formats the bytes of
`frame.data`. In `MockEncoderGoldenCase`, `encoded_frame->data` was
allocated via `base::HeapArray<uint8_t>::Uninit(1920 * 1080)`, causing
MSan to report a read of uninitialized memory.

Use `base::HeapArray<uint8_t>::WithSize(1920 * 1080)` to zero-initialize
`encoded_frame->data` (matching `EncodeOneFrameAndDestroySender`) and
re-enable the test. Verified on Linux.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 500613219

40b53a999a471fafb16d9a7f86733ac919513ff3 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Test that demotion does not re-alert the pinned notification

Lock in the demotion ordering so that the ActorNotificationConfig
refactor cannot regress it. demoteToNonLiveNotification() clears the
task's cached notification before maybeStopServiceNow(), so a repost
during the pin swap finds nothing to repost and the only post is the
silent, non-live demoted notification.

The test pins a finished task through the opt-in notification, the only
path that keeps the service alive during demotion, and checks that every
later post for the task is silent and non-live.

Test-only change.

Ash: Move tablet tuck education nudge to a client-owned widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The tablet tuck education already held its nudge widget and still
destroys it when the nudge is dismissed. The nudge window is now hidden
at once and destroyed a task later, instead of being destroyed at once.

[Optimization Guide Tests] Pin Windows GPU gtests back to GTX 1660

crrev.com/c/8801062 introduced a regression on optimization_guide-win64
CQ and CI by broadening optimization_guide_gpu_gtests to Win11 NVIDIA
RTX 4070 and RTX 5070 bots, where optimization_guide_services_unittests
fails deterministically on LayerNorm fusion.

Pin optimization_guide_gpu_gtests's NVIDIA variant back to Win10 GTX
1660 via WIN10_NVIDIA_GTX_1660, while keeping ondevice_stability_tests,
ondevice_api_scrape_tests, and AMD suites on the Windows 10/11 pools.

R=kenoss@chromium.org

Dictation: Restore text selection from NTP

We require the page context for any selected text. We stopped fetching
it for the NTP due to (1) issues with hangs on other WebUIs and (2) the
introduction of opt guide safety checks. Neither are applicable to the
NTP, so we re-enable page context there, so that selection works again.

tree
a7e11683e2a325da8d6e2fae6f0e6400d2515d8e
parent
b0436cbc932245205716e9746261b455484f5e9a [ diff ] 
swa: Remove g_browser_process from ChromeFileManagerUIDelegate

Remove g_browser_process->GetApplicationLocale() and
g_browser_process->variations_service() from
ChromeFileManagerUIDelegate::GetLoadTimeData() by passing app_locale
and variations_service from AshWebUIConfigManager through
FileManagerUIConfig and FileManagerUI:

  AshWebUIConfigManager
    -> FileManagerUIConfig
      -> FileManagerUI
        -> ChromeFileManagerUIDelegate::GetLoadTimeData

FileManagerUI calls GetLoadTimeData() synchronously during
construction, so neither FileManagerUI nor
ChromeFileManagerUIDelegate retains the parameters. Binding
variations_service_ via base::Unretained in FileManagerUIConfig's
creation lambda is safe because CreateWebUIController() is never
called after BrowserProcessImpl::StartTearDown() destroys
VariationsService.

TAG=agy

1e84419b520fc8551336ca91835f086c1c6756fa [ diff ] 
Ash: Make the virtual trackpad own its widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

A static slot now owns the debug virtual trackpad's widget instead of
keeping a raw pointer to it, and destroys the widget when the trackpad
is toggled off, closed from its frame, or its window goes away.

Multiwindow: Destroy switcher LargeIconBridge

InstanceSwitcherFaviconHelper owns a LargeIconBridge that is never
destroyed when InstanceSwitcherCoordinator or TargetSelectorCoordinator
dismisses its dialog, leaking the native favicon::LargeIconBridge peer.

Add package-private InstanceSwitcherFaviconHelper.destroy() to release
the underlying LargeIconBridge and invoke it from both coordinators'
dialog onDismiss() handlers.

Suggestions: Destroy UI delegate in tile test

MostVisitedTilesLayoutTest constructs a local SuggestionsUiDelegateImpl
during setup without calling onDestroy() in tearDown(), leaking the
LargeIconBridge owned by SuggestionsUiDelegateImpl when
BaseJUnit4ClassRunner checks LifetimeAssert after the test class
finishes.

Retain SuggestionsUiDelegateImpl in a test field and invoke onDestroy()
on the UI thread during tearDown().

device scale, viewport sizing, lifecycle failures, and the disabled flag.

f2764d3f82bb708f96304b61eeb13d81eed28869 [ diff ] 
Use python_binary() for Android, GRIT, and Blink WebIDL build actions

Converts high-frequency / large-dependency Python build actions to
python_binary() and python_action():
- Android Java/Kotlin/dex/resource actions (compile_kt, create_r_java,
  dex, errorprone, turbine; compile_java.py and prepare_resources.py are
  left until siso query ideanalysis and Cronet gn2bp recognize .pyz)
- GRIT actions (grit, pak_util, preprocess_if_expr)
- Blink WebIDL bindings actions (build_web_idl_database,
  check_generated_file_list, collect_idl_files, generate_bindings,
  generate_event_interface_names, validate_web_idl)

Cold single-action runtime (without __pycache__):
  generate_bindings: 255.7ms -> 107.7ms (-58%)
  grit: 208.6ms -> 122.4ms (-41%)
All 814 pak_util, preprocess_if_expr, and create_r_java actions in
chrome_public_apk (-j 32, without __pycache__):
  5.4s -> 4.0s wall (-26%), 156s -> 112s CPU (-28%)

Reland "[WebNN] Switch ML Drift WebGPU from dynamic loading to static linking"

This relands commit 79cfcdfff68e7418e666979339020903dde0cb64
(https://crrev.com/c/8613009).

Changes relative to the original CL (https://crrev.com/c/8613009):
- Keep the symbol extraction and installer packaging targets for
  libLiteRtWebGpuAccelerator.{so,dll,breakpad} in chrome/BUILD.gn,
  chrome/installer/linux/BUILD.gn,
  chrome/installer/linux/common/installer.py, and
  chrome/installer/mini_installer/chrome.release. The original CL was
  reverted because official archive validation in chrome/src-internal
  still expects those files; they will be removed after the internal
  archive configs are updated (https://crrev.com/i/10078847, followed
  by https://crrev.com/c/8761051).
- Add [ Mac14 ] alongside [ Mac15 ] for the crbug.com/572057869
  TestExpectations entries so mac14-tests on CI is also covered without
  un-skipping WebNN tests on Mac13/Mac13-arm64.
- Remove the now-unused base/files/file_util.h include in
  chrome/browser/chrome_content_browser_client.cc.

Additional context on platform scope, binary size, and build changes:
- Desktop platform scope: build_litert_with_ml_drift is intentionally
  enabled on Mac and ChromeOS in addition to Windows and Linux (while
  remaining disabled on Android and iOS). On Mac, it accelerates the
  LiteRT fallback path used in Incognito mode and on Intel Macs where
  CoreML is bypassed. On ChromeOS, webnn_use_litert is already enabled
  and has no OS-native WebNN GPU backend.
- Binary size impact per platform:
  - Linux (x86_64): +4.22 MiB stripped (+4.48 MiB with ThinLTO; +1.66
    MiB gzip) in chrome, offset by -11.41 MiB stripped (-4.59 MiB gzip)
    once standalone libLiteRtWebGpuAccelerator.so is removed in
    https://crrev.com/c/8761051 (net -7.19 MiB uncompressed / -2.93 MiB
    gzip in official packages).
  - Windows: ~3.1-4.5 MiB stripped increase in chrome.dll (arm64 vs.
    x86_64), yielding a net official installer size reduction once
    standalone libLiteRtWebGpuAccelerator.dll is removed in
    https://crrev.com/c/8761051 by deduplicating Abseil, FlatBuffers,
    TFLite, LiteRT, and Dawn.
  - Mac and ChromeOS: ~3.1-4.5 MiB stripped (~1.3-1.7 MiB compressed,
    arm64 vs. x86_64) increase in Google Chrome Framework / chrome,
    where libLiteRtWebGpuAccelerator was not previously shipped.
  - Android and iOS: 0 B.
- //third_party/ml_drift/BUILD.gn: Expose :ml_drift_common_base in
  visibility and add it to :ml_drift public_deps because :ml_drift
  public headers and sources directly include :ml_drift_common_base
  headers (data_type.h, gpu_info.h, shape.h, types.h, etc.), satisfying
  GN check_includes_strict direct-dependency checks
  (crbug.com/555455099).
- WPT TestExpectations: Open-source CQ/CI Win11 (with WebNNOnnxRuntime
  disabled in VirtualTestSuites) and Intel Mac14/Mac15 bots previously
  lacked libLiteRtWebGpuAccelerator and fell back to XNNPACK CPU. Static
  linking exercises the ML Drift WebGPU delegate on those bots for the
  first time, exposing pre-existing delegate precision/op gaps matching
  Linux's existing GPU baselines (rebaselined in
  https://crrev.com/c/8752161).

Original change's description:
> [WebNN] Switch ML Drift WebGPU from dynamic loading to static linking
>
> - Enable build_litert_with_ml_drift on desktop platforms by default in
>   //third_party/litert/features.gni, and add newly rolled
>   fused_sdpa_cache_update sources to //third_party/litert/BUILD.gn.
> - Remove dynamic loading of libLiteRtWebGpuAccelerator.{so,dll},
>   webnn_use_webgpu_accelerator, pre-sandbox preloading hooks
>   (webnn_sandbox_init.{cc,h}), and Linux/Windows installer entries.
> - Temporarily suppress failing virtual/webnn-service-with-gpu WPT
>   tests on Mac15 and Win11 until baselines are updated.
>
> Bug: 572057869
> Compile-Size: Size increase is unavoidable.
> Change-Id: I42c4377da240ad62abb802496c3bb00dc358727d
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8613009
> Reviewed-by: Phillis Tang <phillis@chromium.org>

parent
482a9af39f89fc96eb624c8fcc4d7180ac8e3e58 [ diff ] 
[ServiceWorker] Prefer real workers and skip synthetic response

When an eligible URL is covered by an existing non-synthetic service
worker registration, synthetic response lookup and request interception
must yield to the real worker:

1. Registration lookup (`ServiceWorkerRegistry`):
   Previously, `FindRegistrationForClientUrl()` created a synthetic
   registration before checking `no_registration` or querying storage on
   cache misses. That allowed synthetic registrations to shadow stored
   real service workers when `registration_scope_cache_` or
   `registration_id_cache_` missed, and inserted negative synthetic IDs
   into `registration_id_cache_`. Set `no_registration` on
   `registration_scope_cache_` misses only when the URL is eligible for
   synthetic response and `!MaybeHasRegistrationForStorageKey()`, and
   fall back to a synthetic registration in
   `DidFindRegistrationForClientUrl()` only after storage and
   installing-registration lookups return not found.

2. Request interception (`ServiceWorkerControlleeRequestHandler` and
   `ServiceWorkerMainResourceLoader`):
   Restrict synthetic response interception in
   `ContinueWithActivatedVersion()` and
   `MaybeStartSyntheticNetworkRequest()` to synthetic registrations
   (`id <= kSyntheticResponseServiceWorkerRegistrationId`). Real workers
   with no or empty fetch handlers still bypass loader creation via
   `CompleteWithoutLoader()`, and real workers reaching
   `ServiceWorkerMainResourceLoader` do not have embedder preloads
   intercepted, fetch handlers bypassed, or controlled responses marked
   with `from_synthetic_response`.

8c893429cff3baaa568e645f9a28dcbaa81484e2
parent
c76e04a3aa3c6599640e5e695c391686c329b332 [ diff ] 
[rust png] Cut PNG decode and encode time 4-35% on ChromeOS

Build the `png` and `fdeflate` crates with `optimize_speed` instead
of `optimize`. `optimize` follows the platform default, which for
Rust is size on four platforms and speed on the rest. So only those
four change.

Rust optimization level (`-Copt-level`):

                                           before  after
  ChromeOS, 32-bit Android, Cast, Fuchsia  s       3
  All other platforms                      3       3

Time saved on two ChromeOS devices (encode at the default
compression level):

  x86-64, octopus (Celeron N4020):  decode 5-35%, encode 9-31%
  arm64, corsola (MT8186) big core: decode 7-18%, encode 4-19%
  arm64, same device, little core:  decode 5-13%, encode 7-22%

Code size cost:

  ChromeOS x86-64 (benchmark binary)  14-15 KB
  ChromeOS arm64 (benchmark binary)   7-8 KB
  32-bit Android (size trybot)        8,140 bytes
  64-bit Android (size trybot)        0

Bug: None

d790a2b594eed2b73bb6091de6fe2cd781b279ae [ diff ] 
Migrate DLP and Projector unittests to UserSessionTestEnvironment

DlpReportingManagerTest.UserType and ProjectorUtilsTest used
FakeChromeUserManager, and called RemoveUserFromList() on the logged in
user as an alternative of logging out. Removing a logged in user is not
a supported operation, and is going to be prohibited.

This CL migrates these tests to UserSessionTestEnvironment, which uses
UserManagerImpl and SessionManager, and logs in only one user per test.

- DlpReportingManagerTest.UserType is split into a test for each user
  type.
- In ProjectorUtilsTest, the device local account users are created with
  proper user ids. The demo account case is replaced by a public account,
  because it is what the test intends to check, i.e. a user without
  Gaia account.

Bug: 583613638
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter='DlpReportingManager*:ProjectorUtils*'

[User Ed] Add FeatureProperties to wouldTriggerHelpUi

Adds a Tracker#wouldTriggerHelpUi(String, FeatureProperties) overload
and deprecates Tracker#wouldTriggerHelpUi(String). Shares condition
evaluation logic (screenshot mode, native feature rules, enterprise
policy, and format cooldown groups) with shouldTriggerHelpUi without
recording metrics or mutating cooldown state.

DevTools: allow minimum expiry for blocked response cookies

Max-Age <= 0 produces Time::Min(), including for blocked response
cookies. Allow it in BuildCookie while retaining the Time::Max()
check, so reporting cookie metadata does not trigger a DCHECK.

[Omnibox] Add OmniboxStatefulAiModeButton feature flag

Adds a disabled-by-default feature, exposed to Java and in
chrome://flags as #omnibox-stateful-ai-mode-button, that will make the
AI Mode button in the desktop Android omnibox stateful: it stays visible
in AI Mode and turns into a send button once there is something to
submit.

5604ef6ff45e8b38091df09f1e1efecc47b276a6 [ diff ] 
[Contextual Tasks] Add IsContextualTasksFormFactorSupported()

Follow-up to crrev.com/c/8786381. Adds one helper for "Contextual Tasks
UI is supported on this form factor" (all desktop platforms, and desktop
form factor Android) and uses it in both product code and tests:

- IsContextualTasksUIEnabled() and the CHECK in
  ContextualTasksPanelHost::Create() use the helper.
- Test fixtures skip with SKIP_IF_CONTEXTUAL_TASKS_UNSUPPORTED() (new
  contextual_tasks_test_utils.h) instead of #if BUILDFLAG(IS_ANDROID) +
  device_info::is_desktop() blocks, which also removes the drift between
  is_desktop() and the form factor.
- TearDown() guards use IsSkipped(), except
  ContextualTasksUiServiceTestBase, whose parameterized tests can skip
  from the test body after a full SetUp().

No behavior change.

[SODA] Refactor available_languages_ to constexpr fixed_flat_maps

- Replace the per-instance available_languages_ flat_map and
  ConstructAvailableLanguages() with compile-time
  kDefaultAvailableLanguages and kConchAvailableLanguages fixed_flat_map
  tables.
- Add a lookup helper that checks kConchAvailableLanguages first when
  Conch languages are enabled and falls back to
  kDefaultAvailableLanguages to avoid duplicating unchanged entries.
